Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\exists x_{2}\, :\, \iota,\; \exists h\text{ proving }(x_{2} \in \{x^{'}\in \iota \mid x^{'} \ne x\}),\; (x_{2}\, :\, \{x^{'}\in \iota \mid x^{'} \ne x\}) \in s^{'} \land x_{2} = a_{1}) = (\exists x_{2}\text{ proving }(a_{1} \in \{x^{'}\in \iota \mid x^{'} \ne x\}),\; (a_{1}\, :\, \{x^{'}\in \iota \mid x^{'} \ne x\}) \in s^{'})\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\exists x_{2}\, :\, \{x^{'}\in \iota \mid x^{'} \ne x\},\; x_{2} \in s^{'} \land x_{2} = a_{1}) = (\exists x_{2}\text{ proving }(a_{1} \in \{x^{'}\in \iota \mid x^{'} \ne x\}),\; (a_{1}\, :\, \{x^{'}\in \iota \mid x^{'} \ne x\}) \in s^{'})\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((a_{1} \in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]) = (\exists x_{2}\text{ proving }(a_{1} \in \{x^{'}\in \iota \mid x^{'} \ne x\}),\; (a_{1}\, :\, \{x^{'}\in \iota \mid x^{'} \ne x\}) \in s^{'})\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((a_{1} \in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}] \land x_{1} \in t(a_{1})) = ((\exists x_{2}\text{ proving }(a_{1} \in \{x^{'}\in \iota \mid x^{'} \ne x\}),\; (a_{1}\, :\, \{x^{'}\in \iota \mid x^{'} \ne x\}) \in s^{'}) \land x_{1} \in t(a_{1}))\).
\end{proofstep}
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6269556e696f6e}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6269556e696f6e}}, \paperlink{premise:536574436f652e657869737473}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:536574436f652e657869737473}}, \paperlink{premise:66756e657874}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:66756e657874}}, \paperlink{premise:6966665f73656c66}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6966665f73656c66}} and the two identities established above gives \(x_{1} \in \bigcup_{x_{2}\in s^{'}} t(x_{2}) \iff x_{1} \in \bigcup_{i\in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]} t(i)\).
\end{proofstep}
By \paperlink{premise:537562747970652e636f655f696e6a656374697665}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:537562747970652e636f655f696e6a656374697665}}, we obtain \(\left(a_{1} \mapsto a_{1}\right)\text{ is injective}\).
By \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f696d6167655f6f665f696e6a656374697665}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f696d6167655f6f665f696e6a656374697665}} with \(\{x^{'}\in \iota \mid x^{'} \ne x\}\) as its type parameter \(\alpha\) and \(s = s^{'}\), we obtain \(\left|\left(a_{1} \mapsto a_{1}\right)\left[s^{'}\right]\right| = \left|s^{'}\right|\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((s^{'}\ne\varnothing \implies (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}] \ne \iota \implies |(a_{3} \mapsto a_{3})[s^{'}]| < |\bigcup_{i\in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]} t(i)|) = (s^{'}\ne\varnothing \implies (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}] \ne \iota \implies |s^{'}| < |\bigcup_{i\in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]} t(i)|)\).
Applying the hypothesis \(\forall s\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota),\; s\ne\varnothing \implies s \ne \iota \implies |s| < |\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)|\), we obtain \((z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]\ne\varnothing \implies (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}] \ne \iota \implies |(z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]| < |\bigcup_{i\in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]} t(i)|\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e696d6167655f6e6f6e656d707479}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e696d6167655f6e6f6e656d707479}} in the preceding result gives \(s^{'}\ne\varnothing \implies (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}] \ne \iota \implies |(z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]| < |\bigcup_{i\in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]} t(i)|\).
Use the hypothesis \(s^{'}\ne\varnothing\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(\left(z \mapsto z\right)\left[s^{'}\right] = \iota\).
The substitution uses the following argument.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(a_{1}\) in \(\iota\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(\neg \left(a_{1} = x\right)\).
By definition, we obtain \(((a_{1}\, :\, \{a_{2}\in \iota \mid \neg (a_{2} = x)\}) \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = x) = ((a_{1}\, :\, \{a_{2}\in \iota \mid \neg (a_{2} = x)\}) \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = x)\).
By \paperlink{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}}, we obtain \((a_{1}\, :\, \{a_{2}\in \iota \mid \neg (a_{2} = x)\}) \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = x \iff (a_{1}\, :\, \{a_{2}\in \iota \mid \neg (a_{2} = x)\}) \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = x\).
\end{proofstep}
By definition, we obtain \(\left(\neg \left(a_{1} = x\right)\right) = \left(\neg \left(a_{1} = x\right)\right)\).
By \paperlink{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}}, we obtain \(\neg \left(a_{1} = x\right) \iff \neg \left(a_{1} = x\right)\).
By \paperlink{premise:6578697374735f70726f705f636f6e6772}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6578697374735f70726f705f636f6e6772}}, we obtain \((\exists b\text{ proving }(\neg (a_{1} = x)),\; (a_{1}\, :\, \{a_{2}\in \iota \mid \neg (a_{2} = x)\}) \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = x) \iff (\exists h_{1}\text{ proving }(\neg (a_{1} = x)),\; (a_{1}\, :\, \{a_{2}\in \iota \mid \neg (a_{2} = x)\}) \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = x)\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\exists b\text{ proving }(\neg (a_{1} = x)),\; (a_{1}\, :\, \{a_{2}\in \iota \mid \neg (a_{2} = x)\}) \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = x) = (\exists h_{1}\text{ proving }(\neg (a_{1} = x)),\; (a_{1}\, :\, \{a_{2}\in \iota \mid \neg (a_{2} = x)\}) \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = x)\).
By \paperlink{premise:6578697374735f616e645f7269676874}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6578697374735f616e645f7269676874}} with \(\neg \left(a_{1} = x\right)\) as its type parameter \(\alpha\), we obtain \((\exists x_{1}\, :\, \neg (a_{1} = x),\; (x_{2} \mapsto (a_{1}\, :\, \{a_{2}\in \iota \mid \neg (a_{2} = x)\}) \in s^{'})(x_{1}) \land a_{1} = x) \iff (\exists x_{1}\, :\, \neg (a_{1} = x),\; (x_{2} \mapsto (a_{1}\, :\, \{a_{2}\in \iota \mid \neg (a_{2} = x)\}) \in s^{'})(x_{1})) \land a_{1} = x\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\exists x_{1}\text{ proving }(\neg (a_{1} = x)),\; (a_{1}\, :\, \{a_{2}\in \iota \mid \neg (a_{2} = x)\}) \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = x) = ((\exists x_{1}\text{ proving }(\neg (a_{1} = x)),\; (a_{1}\, :\, \{a_{2}\in \iota \mid \neg (a_{2} = x)\}) \in s^{'}) \land a_{1} = x)\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\exists b\text{ proving }(\neg (a_{1} = x)),\; (a_{1}\, :\, \{a_{2}\in \iota \mid \neg (a_{2} = x)\}) \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = x) = ((\exists x_{1}\text{ proving }(\neg (a_{1} = x)),\; (a_{1}\, :\, \{a_{2}\in \iota \mid \neg (a_{2} = x)\}) \in s^{'}) \land a_{1} = x)\).
\end{proofstep}
The substitution uses the following argument.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(\neg \left(x = x\right)\).
By definition, we obtain \(((x\, :\, \{a_{1}\in \iota \mid \neg (a_{1} = x)\}) \in s^{'}) = ((x\, :\, \{a_{1}\in \iota \mid \neg (a_{1} = x)\}) \in s^{'})\).
By \paperlink{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}}, we obtain \((x\, :\, \{a_{1}\in \iota \mid \neg (a_{1} = x)\}) \in s^{'} \iff (x\, :\, \{a_{1}\in \iota \mid \neg (a_{1} = x)\}) \in s^{'}\).
\end{proofstep}
By \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f756e6976}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f756e6976}}, we obtain \(x \in \iota\).
Rewriting with \(\left(z \mapsto z\right)\left[s^{'}\right] = \iota\), \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f696d616765}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f696d616765}}, \paperlink{premise:537562747970652e657869737473}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:537562747970652e657869737473}}, \paperlink{premise:66756e657874}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:66756e657874}}, \paperlink{premise:6578697374735f65715f7269676874}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6578697374735f65715f7269676874}}, \paperlink{premise:6578697374735f70726f705f636f6e6772}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6578697374735f70726f705f636f6e6772}}, \paperlink{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}}, \paperlink{premise:6e6f745f747275655f65715f66616c7365}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6e6f745f747275655f65715f66616c7365}}, \paperlink{premise:4973456d7074792e6578697374735f696666}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4973456d7074792e6578697374735f696666}}, \paperlink{premise:696e73744973456d70747946616c7365}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:696e73744973456d70747946616c7365}}, the identity established above and the auxiliary result established above in the preceding result gives \(\mathrm{False}\).
The contradiction rules out this case. Hence \(\mathrm{False}\).
\end{proofstep}
Substituting along the equality, we obtain \(\left|s^{'}\right| < \left|\bigcup_{i\in \left(z \mapsto z\right)\left[s^{'}\right]} t(i)\right|\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:65715f6f665f686571}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:65715f6f665f686571}}, \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e657874}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e657874}} and the 7 auxiliary results established above in the preceding result gives \(\left|s^{'}\right| < \left|\bigcup_{x_{1}\in s^{'}} t\left(x_{1}\right)\right|\).
Distinguish whether \(a \in \bigcup_{x_{1}\in s^{'}} t\left(x_{1}\right)\) holds.
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(a \in \bigcup_{x_{1}\in s^{'}} t\left(x_{1}\right)\).
Use the previously proved fact \(\left|s^{'}\right| < \left|\bigcup_{x_{1}\in s^{'}} t\left(x_{1}\right)\right|\).
By \paperlink{premise:4e61742e6c655f7375625f6f6e655f6f665f6c74}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e6c655f7375625f6f6e655f6f665f6c74}}, we obtain \(\left|s^{'}\right| \le \left|\bigcup_{x_{1}\in s^{'}} t\left(x_{1}\right)\right| - 1\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f65726173655f6f665f6d656d}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f65726173655f6f665f6d656d}} (using \(a \in \bigcup_{x_{1}\in s^{'}} t\left(x_{1}\right)\)) in the preceding result gives \(\left|s^{'}\right| \le \left|\left(\bigcup_{x^{'}\in s^{'}} t\left(x^{'}\right)\right) \setminus \{a\}\right|\).
\end{proofstep}
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(a \notin \bigcup_{x_{1}\in s^{'}} t\left(x_{1}\right)\).
Use the previously proved fact \(\left|s^{'}\right| < \left|\bigcup_{x_{1}\in s^{'}} t\left(x_{1}\right)\right|\).
By \paperlink{premise:4e61742e6c655f6f665f6c74}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e6c655f6f665f6c74}}, we obtain \(\left|s^{'}\right| \le \left|\bigcup_{x_{1}\in s^{'}} t\left(x_{1}\right)\right|\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e65726173655f65715f6f665f6e6f745f6d656d}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e65726173655f65715f6f665f6e6f745f6d656d}} (using \(a \notin \bigcup_{x_{1}\in s^{'}} t\left(x_{1}\right)\)) in the preceding result gives \(\left|s^{'}\right| \le \left|\left(\bigcup_{x^{'}\in s^{'}} t\left(x^{'}\right)\right) \setminus \{a\}\right|\).
\end{proofstep}
The proposition or its negation holds. Hence \(\left|s^{'}\right| \le \left|\left(\bigcup_{x^{'}\in s^{'}} t\left(x^{'}\right)\right) \setminus \{a\}\right|\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e65726173655f6269556e696f6e}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e65726173655f6269556e696f6e}} in the preceding result gives \(\left|s^{'}\right| \le \left|\bigcup_{x^{'}\in s^{'}} \left(t\left(x^{'}\right) \setminus \{a\}\right)\right|\).
\end{proofstep}
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(\neg \left(s^{'}\ne\varnothing\right)\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(\varnothing\ne\varnothing \implies (z \mapsto z)[\varnothing] \ne \iota \implies |\varnothing| < |\bigcup_{i\in (z \mapsto z)[\varnothing]} t(i)|\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:6c655f7265666c}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6c655f7265666c}} gives \(0 \le 0\).
\end{proofstep}
Use the hypothesis \(\neg \left(s^{'}\ne\varnothing\right)\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6e6f6e656d7074795f6966665f6e655f656d707479}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6e6f6e656d7074795f6966665f6e655f656d707479}} in the preceding result gives \(\neg \left(\neg \left(s^{'} = \varnothing\right)\right)\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:436c6173736963616c2e6e6f745f6e6f74}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:436c6173736963616c2e6e6f745f6e6f74}} in the preceding result gives \(s^{'} = \varnothing\).
Reversing the equality, we obtain \(\varnothing = s^{'}\).
Applying the hypothesis \(\forall s\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota),\; s\ne\varnothing \implies s \ne \iota \implies |s| < |\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)|\), we obtain \((z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]\ne\varnothing \implies (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}] \ne \iota \implies |(z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]| < |\bigcup_{i\in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]} t(i)|\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e696d6167655f6e6f6e656d707479}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e696d6167655f6e6f6e656d707479}} in the preceding result gives \(s^{'}\ne\varnothing \implies (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}] \ne \iota \implies |(a_{3} \mapsto a_{3})[s^{'}]| < |\bigcup_{i\in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]} t(i)|\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f696d6167655f6f665f696e6a656374697665}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f696d6167655f6f665f696e6a656374697665}} and \paperlink{premise:537562747970652e636f655f696e6a656374697665}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:537562747970652e636f655f696e6a656374697665}} in the preceding result gives \(s^{'}\ne\varnothing \implies (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}] \ne \iota \implies |s^{'}| < |\bigcup_{i\in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]} t(i)|\).
Substituting equals, we obtain \(\left|s^{'}\right| \le \left|\bigcup_{x^{'}\in s^{'}} \left(t\left(x^{'}\right) \setminus \{a\}\right)\right|\).
\end{proofstep}
The proposition or its negation holds. Hence \(\left|s^{'}\right| \le \left|\bigcup_{x^{'}\in s^{'}} \left(t\left(x^{'}\right) \setminus \{a\}\right)\right|\).
\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}[{First case of the inductive step}]\label{theorem:2}
Let \(\iota\) and \(\alpha\) be types. Assume equality on \(\alpha\) is decidable. Let \(t\) be a family of finite subsets of \(\alpha\) indexed by \(\iota\). Fix a finite enumeration of \(\iota\). Let \(n\) be a natural number. Assume \(\left|\iota\right| = n + 1\). Assume \(\forall s\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota),\; \left|s\right| \le \left|\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)\right|\). Assume as induction hypothesis that \(\forall \iota^{'}\text{ with finite enumeration }\mathit{inst}_{2},\; \forall t^{'}\, :\, \iota^{'} \to \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\alpha),\; |\iota^{'}| \le n \implies (\forall s^{'}\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota^{'}),\; |s^{'}| \le |\bigcup_{i\in s^{'}} t^{'}(i)|) \implies (\exists f\, :\, \iota^{'} \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota^{'},\; f(x) \in t^{'}(x)))\). Assume \(\forall s\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota),\; s\ne\varnothing \implies s \ne \iota \implies |s| < |\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)|\). Then
\begin{center}
\(\displaystyle \exists f\, :\, \iota \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota,\; f(x) \in t(x)).\)
\end{center}
\end{lemma}

\begin{proof}
Set \(x\) equal to \(\text{a fixed element of }\iota\).
By \paperlink{premise:46696e747970652e636172645f706f735f696666}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e747970652e636172645f706f735f696666}}, we obtain \(0 < \left|\iota\right| \iff \iota\text{ is nonempty}\).
By \paperlink{premise:4e61742e737563635f706f73}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e737563635f706f73}}, we obtain \(0 < n + 1\).
Use the hypothesis \(\left|\iota\right| = n + 1\).
Reversing the equality, we obtain \(n + 1 = \left|\iota\right|\).
Substituting equals, we obtain \(0 < \left|\iota\right|\).
By the forward implication, we obtain \(\iota\text{ is nonempty}\).
Zero is less than one, we obtain \(0 < 1\).
Applying the hypothesis \(\forall s\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota),\; \left|s\right| \le \left|\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)\right|\), we obtain \(\left|\{x\}\right| \le \left|\bigcup_{i\in \{x\}} t(i)\right|\).
By transitivity of the displayed relations, we obtain \(0 < \left|\bigcup_{i\in \{x\}} t(i)\right|\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e73696e676c65746f6e5f6269556e696f6e}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e73696e676c65746f6e5f6269556e696f6e}} gives \(\left|\bigcup_{i\in \{x\}} t(i)\right| = \left|t\left(x\right)\right|\).
By transitivity of the displayed relations, we obtain \(0 < \left|t\left(x\right)\right|\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f706f73}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f706f73}} in the preceding result gives \(t\left(x\right)\ne\varnothing\).
Fix \(x_{1}\) such that \(x_{1} \in t\left(x\right)\).
Set \(\iota^{'}\) equal to \(\{x^{'}\in \iota \mid x^{'} \ne x\}\).
Set \(t^{'}\) equal to \(x^{'} \mapsto t\left(x^{'}\right) \setminus \{x_{1}\}\).
By \paperlink{premise:5365742e636172645f6e655f6571}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:5365742e636172645f6e655f6571}} with \(a = x\), we obtain \(\left|\{x_{2}\in \iota \mid x_{2} \ne x\}\right| = \left|\iota\right| - 1\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:6164645f7a65726f}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6164645f7a65726f}}, \paperlink{premise:4e61742e6164645f737563635f7375625f6f6e65}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e6164645f737563635f7375625f6f6e65}} and \(\left|\iota\right| = n + 1\) gives \(\left|\iota\right| - 1 = n\).
By transitivity of the displayed relations, we obtain \(\left|\iota^{'}\right| = n\).
Use the previously proved fact \(\left|\iota^{'}\right| = n\).
By \paperlink{premise:45712e6c65}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:45712e6c65}} with \(\mathbb{N}\) as its type parameter \(\alpha\), we obtain \(\left|\iota^{'}\right| \le n\).
By \paperlink{theorem:1}{Lemma~\ref{theorem:1}} with \(a = x_{1}\), we obtain \(\forall s^{'}\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\{x^{'}\in \iota \mid x^{'} \ne x\}),\; |s^{'}| \le |\bigcup_{x^{'}\in s^{'}} (t(x^{'}) \setminus \{x_{1}\})|\).
Applying the induction hypothesis, we obtain \(\exists f\, :\, \iota^{'} \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x_{2}\, :\, \iota^{'},\; f(x_{2}) \in t^{'}(x_{2}))\).
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(f^{'}\) in \(\iota^{'} \to \alpha\).
Assume \(f^{'}\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x_{2}\, :\, \iota^{'},\; f^{'}(x_{2}) \in t^{'}(x_{2}))\).
Use the hypothesis \(f^{'}\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x_{2}\, :\, \iota^{'},\; f^{'}(x_{2}) \in t^{'}(x_{2}))\).
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(f^{'}\text{ is injective}\).
Assume \(\forall x_{2}\, :\, \iota^{'},\; f^{'}\left(x_{2}\right) \in t^{'}\left(x_{2}\right)\).
Choose the witness \(z \mapsto \begin{cases}x_{1} &\text{if } z = x\\ f^{'}((z\, :\, \iota^{'})) &\text{given } h_{1}\text{ proving } \neg (z = x)\end{cases}\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(z_{1}\) in \(\iota\).
Fix \(z_{2}\) in \(\iota\).
Fix \(x_{2}\) in \(\iota^{'}\).
Assume \(x_{1} = f^{'}\left(x_{2}\right)\).
Applying the hypothesis \(\forall x_{2}\, :\, \iota^{'},\; f^{'}\left(x_{2}\right) \in t^{'}\left(x_{2}\right)\), we obtain \(f^{'}\left(x_{2}\right) \in t^{'}\left(x_{2}\right)\).
Rewriting with \(x_{1} = f^{'}\left(x_{2}\right)\), \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6572617365}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6572617365}}, \paperlink{premise:6e6f745f747275655f65715f66616c7365}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6e6f745f747275655f65715f66616c7365}} and \paperlink{premise:66616c73655f616e64}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:66616c73655f616e64}} in the preceding result gives \(\mathrm{False}\).
The contradiction rules out this case. Hence \(\mathrm{False}\).
Distinguish whether \(z_{1} = x\) holds.
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(z_{1} = x\).
Distinguish whether \(z_{2} = x\) holds.
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(z_{2} = x\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:696d706c6965735f636f6e6772}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:696d706c6965735f636f6e6772}}, \paperlink{premise:646974655f636f6e645f65715f74727565}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:646974655f636f6e645f65715f74727565}}, \(z_{1} = x\), \(z_{2} = x\) and \paperlink{premise:696d705f73656c66}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:696d705f73656c66}} gives \(\begin{cases}x_{1} &\text{if } z_{1} = x\\ f^{'}((z_{1}\, :\, \iota^{'})) &\text{given } h_{3}\text{ proving } \neg (z_{1} = x)\end{cases} = \begin{cases}x_{1} &\text{if } z_{2} = x\\ f^{'}((z_{2}\, :\, \iota^{'})) &\text{given } h_{3}\text{ proving } \neg (z_{2} = x)\end{cases} \implies z_{1} = z_{2}\).
\end{proofstep}
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(\neg \left(z_{2} = x\right)\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:696d706c6965735f636f6e6772}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:696d706c6965735f636f6e6772}}, \paperlink{premise:646974655f636f6e645f65715f74727565}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:646974655f636f6e645f65715f74727565}}, \(z_{1} = x\), \paperlink{premise:646974655f636f6e645f65715f66616c7365}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:646974655f636f6e645f65715f66616c7365}}, \paperlink{premise:65715f66616c7365}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:65715f66616c7365}} (using \(\neg \left(z_{2} = x\right)\)), \paperlink{premise:65715f66616c7365}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:65715f66616c7365}}, \(\forall x_{2}\, :\, \iota^{'},\; x_{1} \ne f^{'}\left(x_{2}\right)\), \paperlink{premise:4973456d7074792e666f72616c6c5f696666}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4973456d7074792e666f72616c6c5f696666}} and \paperlink{premise:696e73744973456d70747946616c7365}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:696e73744973456d70747946616c7365}} gives \(\begin{cases}x_{1} &\text{if } z_{1} = x\\ f^{'}((z_{1}\, :\, \iota^{'})) &\text{given } h_{3}\text{ proving } \neg (z_{1} = x)\end{cases} = \begin{cases}x_{1} &\text{if } z_{2} = x\\ f^{'}((z_{2}\, :\, \iota^{'})) &\text{given } h_{3}\text{ proving } \neg (z_{2} = x)\end{cases} \implies z_{1} = z_{2}\).
\end{proofstep}
The proposition or its negation holds. Hence \(\begin{cases}x_{1} &\text{if } z_{1} = x\\ f^{'}((z_{1}\, :\, \iota^{'})) &\text{given } h_{2}\text{ proving } \neg (z_{1} = x)\end{cases} = \begin{cases}x_{1} &\text{if } z_{2} = x\\ f^{'}((z_{2}\, :\, \iota^{'})) &\text{given } h_{2}\text{ proving } \neg (z_{2} = x)\end{cases} \implies z_{1} = z_{2}\).
\end{proofstep}
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(\neg \left(z_{1} = x\right)\).
Distinguish whether \(z_{2} = x\) holds.
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(z_{2} = x\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:696d706c6965735f636f6e6772}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:696d706c6965735f636f6e6772}}, \paperlink{premise:646974655f636f6e645f65715f66616c7365}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:646974655f636f6e645f65715f66616c7365}}, \paperlink{premise:65715f66616c7365}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:65715f66616c7365}} (using \(\neg \left(z_{1} = x\right)\)), \paperlink{premise:646974655f636f6e645f65715f74727565}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:646974655f636f6e645f65715f74727565}}, \(z_{2} = x\), \paperlink{premise:65715f66616c7365}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:65715f66616c7365}}, \paperlink{premise:4e652e73796d6d}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e652e73796d6d}}, \(\forall x_{2}\, :\, \iota^{'},\; x_{1} \ne f^{'}\left(x_{2}\right)\) and \paperlink{premise:696d705f73656c66}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:696d705f73656c66}} gives \(\begin{cases}x_{1} &\text{if } z_{1} = x\\ f^{'}((z_{1}\, :\, \iota^{'})) &\text{given } h_{3}\text{ proving } \neg (z_{1} = x)\end{cases} = \begin{cases}x_{1} &\text{if } z_{2} = x\\ f^{'}((z_{2}\, :\, \iota^{'})) &\text{given } h_{3}\text{ proving } \neg (z_{2} = x)\end{cases} \implies z_{1} = z_{2}\).
\end{proofstep}
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(\neg \left(z_{2} = x\right)\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:696d706c6965735f636f6e6772}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:696d706c6965735f636f6e6772}}, \paperlink{premise:646974655f636f6e645f65715f66616c7365}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:646974655f636f6e645f65715f66616c7365}}, \paperlink{premise:65715f66616c7365}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:65715f66616c7365}} (using \(\neg \left(z_{1} = x\right)\)), \paperlink{premise:65715f66616c7365}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:65715f66616c7365}} (using \(\neg \left(z_{2} = x\right)\)), \paperlink{premise:46756e6374696f6e2e496e6a6563746976652e65715f696666}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46756e6374696f6e2e496e6a6563746976652e65715f696666}} (using \(f^{'}\text{ is injective}\)), \paperlink{premise:537562747970652e6d6b2e696e6a4571}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:537562747970652e6d6b2e696e6a4571}}, \paperlink{premise:6f665f65715f66616c7365}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6f665f65715f66616c7365}} and \paperlink{premise:696d705f73656c66}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:696d705f73656c66}} gives \(\begin{cases}x_{1} &\text{if } z_{1} = x\\ f^{'}((z_{1}\, :\, \iota^{'})) &\text{given } h_{3}\text{ proving } \neg (z_{1} = x)\end{cases} = \begin{cases}x_{1} &\text{if } z_{2} = x\\ f^{'}((z_{2}\, :\, \iota^{'})) &\text{given } h_{3}\text{ proving } \neg (z_{2} = x)\end{cases} \implies z_{1} = z_{2}\).
\end{proofstep}
The proposition or its negation holds. Hence \(\begin{cases}x_{1} &\text{if } z_{1} = x\\ f^{'}((z_{1}\, :\, \iota^{'})) &\text{given } h_{2}\text{ proving } \neg (z_{1} = x)\end{cases} = \begin{cases}x_{1} &\text{if } z_{2} = x\\ f^{'}((z_{2}\, :\, \iota^{'})) &\text{given } h_{2}\text{ proving } \neg (z_{2} = x)\end{cases} \implies z_{1} = z_{2}\).
\end{proofstep}
The proposition or its negation holds. Hence \(\begin{cases}x_{1} &\text{if } z_{1} = x\\ f^{'}((z_{1}\, :\, \iota^{'})) &\text{given } h_{1}\text{ proving } \neg (z_{1} = x)\end{cases} = \begin{cases}x_{1} &\text{if } z_{2} = x\\ f^{'}((z_{2}\, :\, \iota^{'})) &\text{given } h_{1}\text{ proving } \neg (z_{2} = x)\end{cases} \implies z_{1} = z_{2}\).
\end{proofstep}
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(z\) in \(\iota\).
Distinguish whether \(z = x\) holds.
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(z = x\).
Use the previously proved fact \(t\left(x\right)\ne\varnothing\).
By \paperlink{premise:436c6173736963616c2e63686f6f73655f73706563}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:436c6173736963616c2e63686f6f73655f73706563}}, we obtain \(x_{1} \in t\left(x\right)\).
Rewriting with \(z = x\) in the preceding result gives \(x_{1} \in t\left(z\right)\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:6469665f706f73}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6469665f706f73}} (using \(z = x\)) in the preceding result gives \(\begin{cases}x_{1} &\text{if } z = x\\ f^{'}((z\, :\, \iota^{'})) &\text{given } h_{1}\text{ proving } \neg (z = x)\end{cases} \in t(z)\).
\end{proofstep}
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(\neg \left(z = x\right)\).
Applying the hypothesis \(\forall x_{2}\, :\, \iota^{'},\; f^{'}\left(x_{2}\right) \in t^{'}\left(x_{2}\right)\), we obtain \(f^{'}\left(\left(z\, :\, \iota^{'}\right)\right) \in t\left(z\right) \setminus \{x_{1}\}\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6572617365}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6572617365}} in the preceding result gives \(f^{'}((z\, :\, \iota^{'})) \ne x_{1} \land f^{'}((z\, :\, \iota^{'})) \in t(z)\).
Taking the second conjunct, we obtain \(f^{'}\left(\left(z\, :\, \iota^{'}\right)\right) \in t\left(z\right)\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:6469665f6e6567}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6469665f6e6567}} (using \(\neg \left(z = x\right)\)) in the preceding result gives \(\begin{cases}x_{1} &\text{if } z = x\\ f^{'}((z\, :\, \iota^{'})) &\text{given } h_{1}\text{ proving } \neg (z = x)\end{cases} \in t(z)\).
\end{proofstep}
The proposition or its negation holds. Hence \(\begin{cases}x_{1} &\text{if } z = x\\ f^{'}((z\, :\, \iota^{'})) &\text{given } h_{1}\text{ proving } \neg (z = x)\end{cases} \in t(z)\).
By the preceding argument, we obtain \(\begin{cases}x_{1} &\text{if } z = x\\ f^{'}((z\, :\, \iota^{'})) &\text{given } h_{1}\text{ proving } \neg (z = x)\end{cases} \in t(z)\).
\end{proofstep}
Combining the two conclusions, we obtain \((z \mapsto \begin{cases}x_{1} &\text{if } z = x\\ f^{'}((z\, :\, \iota^{'})) &\text{given } h_{1}\text{ proving } \neg (z = x)\end{cases})\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x_{2}\, :\, \iota,\; \begin{cases}x_{1} &\text{if } x_{2} = x\\ f^{'}((x_{2}\, :\, \iota^{'})) &\text{given } h_{1}\text{ proving } \neg (x_{2} = x)\end{cases} \in t(x_{2}))\).
This witness proves existence. Hence \(\exists f\, :\, \iota \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x_{2}\, :\, \iota,\; f(x_{2}) \in t(x_{2}))\).
\end{proofstep}
Using the two conjuncts, we obtain \((x_{2} \mapsto \exists f\, :\, \iota \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x_{3}\, :\, \iota,\; f(x_{3}) \in t(x_{3})))(h)\).
\end{proofstep}
Using the existential hypothesis, we obtain \(\exists f\, :\, \iota \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x_{2}\, :\, \iota,\; f(x_{2}) \in t(x_{2}))\).
\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}[{Hall condition for a restricted family}]\label{theorem:3}
Let \(\alpha\) be a type. Assume equality on \(\alpha\) is decidable. Let \(\iota\) be a type. Let \(t\) be a family of finite subsets of \(\alpha\) indexed by \(\iota\). Let \(s\) be a finite subset of \(\iota\). Assume \(\forall s_{1}\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota),\; |s_{1}| \le |\bigcup_{i\in s_{1}} t(i)|\). Let \(s^{'}\) be a finite subset of \(s\). Then \(\left|s^{'}\right| \le \left|\bigcup_{a^{'}\in s^{'}} t\left(a^{'}\right)\right|\).
\end{lemma}

\begin{proof}
The substitution uses the following argument.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(y\) in \(\alpha\).
The substitution uses the following argument.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(a\) in \(\iota\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(a \in s\).
By definition, we obtain \(((a\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land y \in t(a)) = ((a\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land y \in t(a))\).
By \paperlink{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}}, we obtain \((a\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land y \in t(a) \iff (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land y \in t(a)\).
\end{proofstep}
By definition, we obtain \(\left(a \in s\right) = \left(a \in s\right)\).
By \paperlink{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}}, we obtain \(a \in s \iff a \in s\).
By \paperlink{premise:6578697374735f70726f705f636f6e6772}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6578697374735f70726f705f636f6e6772}}, we obtain \((\exists b\text{ proving }(a \in s),\; (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land y \in t(a)) \iff (\exists h\text{ proving }(a \in s),\; (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land y \in t(a))\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\exists b\text{ proving }(a \in s),\; (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land y \in t(a)) = (\exists h\text{ proving }(a \in s),\; (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land y \in t(a))\).
By \paperlink{premise:6578697374735f616e645f7269676874}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6578697374735f616e645f7269676874}} with \(a \in s\) as its type parameter \(\alpha\), we obtain \((\exists x\, :\, a \in s,\; (x_{1} \mapsto (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'})(x) \land y \in t(a)) \iff (\exists x\, :\, a \in s,\; (x_{1} \mapsto (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'})(x)) \land y \in t(a)\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\exists x\text{ proving }(a \in s),\; (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land y \in t(a)) = ((\exists x\text{ proving }(a \in s),\; (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'}) \land y \in t(a))\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\exists b\text{ proving }(a \in s),\; (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land y \in t(a)) = ((\exists x\text{ proving }(a \in s),\; (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'}) \land y \in t(a))\).
\end{proofstep}
The substitution uses the following argument.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(a\) in \(\iota\).
By \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f696d616765}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f696d616765}} with \(s\) as its type parameter \(\alpha\), we obtain \(a \in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}] \iff (\exists a_{1}\, :\, s,\; a_{1} \in s^{'} \land (z \mapsto z)(a_{1}) = a)\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((a \in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]) = (\exists a_{1}\, :\, s,\; a_{1} \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = a)\).
By \paperlink{premise:537562747970652e657869737473}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:537562747970652e657869737473}}, we obtain \((\exists x\, :\, s,\; (x_{1} \mapsto x_{1} \in s^{'} \land x_{1} = a)(x)) \iff (\exists a_{1}\, :\, \iota,\; \exists b\text{ proving }(a_{1} \in s),\; (x \mapsto x \in s^{'} \land x = a)((a_{1}\, :\, s)))\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\exists x\, :\, s,\; x \in s^{'} \land x = a) = (\exists a_{1}\, :\, \iota,\; \exists b\text{ proving }(a_{1} \in s),\; (a_{1}\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = a)\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(a_{1}\) in \(\iota\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(a_{1} \in s\).
By definition, we obtain \(((a_{1}\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = a) = ((a_{1}\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = a)\).
By \paperlink{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}}, we obtain \(\left(a_{1}\, :\, s\right) \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = a \iff \left(a_{1}\, :\, s\right) \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = a\).
\end{proofstep}
By definition, we obtain \(\left(a_{1} \in s\right) = \left(a_{1} \in s\right)\).
By \paperlink{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}}, we obtain \(a_{1} \in s \iff a_{1} \in s\).
By \paperlink{premise:6578697374735f70726f705f636f6e6772}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6578697374735f70726f705f636f6e6772}}, we obtain \((\exists b\text{ proving }(a_{1} \in s),\; (a_{1}\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = a) \iff (\exists h\text{ proving }(a_{1} \in s),\; (a_{1}\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = a)\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\exists b\text{ proving }(a_{1} \in s),\; (a_{1}\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = a) = (\exists h\text{ proving }(a_{1} \in s),\; (a_{1}\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = a)\).
By \paperlink{premise:6578697374735f616e645f7269676874}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6578697374735f616e645f7269676874}} with \(a_{1} \in s\) as its type parameter \(\alpha\), we obtain \((\exists x\, :\, a_{1} \in s,\; (x_{1} \mapsto (a_{1}\, :\, s) \in s^{'})(x) \land a_{1} = a) \iff (\exists x\, :\, a_{1} \in s,\; (x_{1} \mapsto (a_{1}\, :\, s) \in s^{'})(x)) \land a_{1} = a\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\exists x\text{ proving }(a_{1} \in s),\; (a_{1}\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = a) = ((\exists x\text{ proving }(a_{1} \in s),\; (a_{1}\, :\, s) \in s^{'}) \land a_{1} = a)\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\exists b\text{ proving }(a_{1} \in s),\; (a_{1}\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = a) = ((\exists x\text{ proving }(a_{1} \in s),\; (a_{1}\, :\, s) \in s^{'}) \land a_{1} = a)\).
\end{proofstep}
By function extensionality, we obtain \((x \mapsto \exists b\text{ proving }(x \in s),\; (x\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land x = a) = (x \mapsto (\exists x_{1}\text{ proving }(x \in s),\; (x\, :\, s) \in s^{'}) \land x = a)\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((\exists x\, :\, \iota,\; \exists b\text{ proving }(x \in s),\; (x\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land x = a) = (\exists x\, :\, \iota,\; (\exists x_{1}\text{ proving }(x \in s),\; (x\, :\, s) \in s^{'}) \land x = a)\).
By \paperlink{premise:6578697374735f65715f7269676874}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6578697374735f65715f7269676874}}, we obtain \((\exists a_{1}\, :\, \iota,\; (a_{2} \mapsto \exists x\text{ proving }(a_{2} \in s),\; (a_{2}\, :\, s) \in s^{'})(a_{1}) \land a_{1} = a) \iff (a_{1} \mapsto \exists x\text{ proving }(a_{1} \in s),\; (a_{1}\, :\, s) \in s^{'})(a)\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\exists a_{1}\, :\, \iota,\; (\exists x\text{ proving }(a_{1} \in s),\; (a_{1}\, :\, s) \in s^{'}) \land a_{1} = a) = (\exists x\text{ proving }(a \in s),\; (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'})\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\exists x\, :\, \iota,\; \exists b\text{ proving }(x \in s),\; (x\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land x = a) = (\exists x\text{ proving }(a \in s),\; (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'})\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\exists x\, :\, s,\; x \in s^{'} \land x = a) = (\exists x\text{ proving }(a \in s),\; (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'})\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((a \in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]) = (\exists x\text{ proving }(a \in s),\; (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'})\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((a \in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}] \land y \in t(a)) = ((\exists x\text{ proving }(a \in s),\; (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'}) \land y \in t(a))\).
\end{proofstep}
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6269556e696f6e}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6269556e696f6e}}, \paperlink{premise:537562747970652e657869737473}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:537562747970652e657869737473}}, \paperlink{premise:66756e657874}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:66756e657874}}, \paperlink{premise:6966665f73656c66}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6966665f73656c66}} and the two identities established above gives \(y \in \bigcup_{a^{'}\in s^{'}} t(a^{'}) \iff y \in \bigcup_{i\in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]} t(i)\).
\end{proofstep}
The substitution uses the following argument.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(|\bigcup_{i\in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]} t(i)| = |\bigcup_{a^{'}\in s^{'}} t(a^{'})|\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(|\bigcup_{a^{'}\in s^{'}} t(a^{'})| = |\bigcup_{a^{'}\in s^{'}} t(a^{'})|\).
Assume \(\text{the proof of }(|\bigcup_{a^{'}\in s^{'}} t(a^{'})| = |\bigcup_{a^{'}\in s^{'}} t(a^{'})|)\text{ agrees under transport with the proof of }(|\bigcup_{a^{'}\in s^{'}} t(a^{'})| = |\bigcup_{a^{'}\in s^{'}} t(a^{'})|)\).
By reflexivity, we obtain \(|(a \mapsto a)[s^{'}]| \le |\bigcup_{a^{'}\in s^{'}} t(a^{'})|\) heterogeneously equal to itself.
\end{proofstep}
Use the hypothesis \(|\bigcup_{i\in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]} t(i)| = |\bigcup_{a^{'}\in s^{'}} t(a^{'})|\).
Reversing the equality, we obtain \(|\bigcup_{a^{'}\in s^{'}} t(a^{'})| = |\bigcup_{i\in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]} t(i)|\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(y\) in \(\alpha\).
The substitution uses the following argument.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(a\) in \(\iota\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(a \in s\).
By definition, we obtain \(((a\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land y \in t(a)) = ((a\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land y \in t(a))\).
By \paperlink{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}}, we obtain \((a\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land y \in t(a) \iff (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land y \in t(a)\).
\end{proofstep}
By definition, we obtain \(\left(a \in s\right) = \left(a \in s\right)\).
By \paperlink{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}}, we obtain \(a \in s \iff a \in s\).
By \paperlink{premise:6578697374735f70726f705f636f6e6772}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6578697374735f70726f705f636f6e6772}}, we obtain \((\exists b\text{ proving }(a \in s),\; (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land y \in t(a)) \iff (\exists h_{1}\text{ proving }(a \in s),\; (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land y \in t(a))\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\exists b\text{ proving }(a \in s),\; (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land y \in t(a)) = (\exists h_{1}\text{ proving }(a \in s),\; (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land y \in t(a))\).
By \paperlink{premise:6578697374735f616e645f7269676874}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6578697374735f616e645f7269676874}} with \(a \in s\) as its type parameter \(\alpha\), we obtain \((\exists x\, :\, a \in s,\; (x_{1} \mapsto (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'})(x) \land y \in t(a)) \iff (\exists x\, :\, a \in s,\; (x_{1} \mapsto (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'})(x)) \land y \in t(a)\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\exists x\text{ proving }(a \in s),\; (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land y \in t(a)) = ((\exists x\text{ proving }(a \in s),\; (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'}) \land y \in t(a))\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\exists b\text{ proving }(a \in s),\; (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land y \in t(a)) = ((\exists x\text{ proving }(a \in s),\; (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'}) \land y \in t(a))\).
\end{proofstep}

By function extensionality, we obtain \((x \mapsto \exists b\text{ proving }(x \in s),\; (x\, :\, s) \in s^{'} \land x = a) = (x \mapsto (\exists x_{1}\text{ proving }(x \in s),\; (x\, :\, s) \in s^{'}) \land x = a)\).

By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\exists a_{1}\, :\, \iota,\; (\exists x\text{ proving }(a_{1} \in s),\; (a_{1}\, :\, s) \in s^{'}) \land a_{1} = a) = (\exists x\text{ proving }(a \in s),\; (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'})\).

Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\exists x\, :\, s,\; x \in s^{'} \land x = a) = (\exists x\text{ proving }(a \in s),\; (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'})\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((a \in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]) = (\exists x\text{ proving }(a \in s),\; (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'})\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((a \in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}] \land y \in t(a)) = ((\exists x\text{ proving }(a \in s),\; (a\, :\, s) \in s^{'}) \land y \in t(a))\).
\end{proofstep}

Substituting equals, we obtain \(\text{the proof of }(|\bigcup_{a^{'}\in s^{'}} t(a^{'})| = |\bigcup_{i\in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]} t(i)|)\text{ agrees under transport with the proof of }(|\bigcup_{a^{'}\in s^{'}} t(a^{'})| = |\bigcup_{a^{'}\in s^{'}} t(a^{'})|) \implies (|(a \mapsto a)[s^{'}]| \le |\bigcup_{a^{'}\in s^{'}} t(a^{'})|\, :\, \mathsf{Prop})\text{ is heterogeneously equal to }(|(a \mapsto a)[s^{'}]| \le |\bigcup_{i\in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]} t(i)|\, :\, \mathsf{Prop})\).
\end{proofstep}

\begin{lemma}[{Hall condition on the complement}]\label{theorem:4}
Let \(\alpha\) be a type. Assume equality on \(\alpha\) is decidable. Let \(\iota\) be a type. Let \(t\) be a family of finite subsets of \(\alpha\) indexed by \(\iota\). Let \(s\) be a finite subset of \(\iota\). Assume \(\left|s\right| = \left|\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)\right|\). Assume \(\forall s_{1}\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota),\; |s_{1}| \le |\bigcup_{i\in s_{1}} t(i)|\). Let \(s^{'}\) be a finite subset of \(s^{\mathrm c}\). Then
\begin{center}
\(\displaystyle |s^{'}| \le |\bigcup_{x^{'}\in s^{'}} (t(x^{'}) \setminus (\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)))|.\)
\end{center}
\end{lemma}

\begin{proof}
The substitution uses the following argument.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(a\) in \(\iota\).
By definition, we obtain \(\left(a \in s\right) = \left(a \in s\right)\).
By \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f696d616765}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f696d616765}} with \(s^{\mathrm c}\) as its type parameter \(\alpha\), we obtain \(a \in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}] \iff (\exists a_{1}\, :\, s^{\mathrm c},\; a_{1} \in s^{'} \land (z \mapsto z)(a_{1}) = a)\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((a \in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]) = (\exists a_{1}\, :\, s^{\mathrm c},\; a_{1} \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = a)\).
By \paperlink{premise:536574436f652e657869737473}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:536574436f652e657869737473}}, we obtain \((\exists x\, :\, s^{\mathrm c},\; (x_{1} \mapsto x_{1} \in s^{'} \land x_{1} = a)(x)) \iff (\exists x\, :\, \iota,\; \exists h\text{ proving }(x \in s^{\mathrm c}),\; (x_{1} \mapsto x_{1} \in s^{'} \land x_{1} = a)((x\, :\, s^{\mathrm c})))\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\exists x\, :\, s^{\mathrm c},\; x \in s^{'} \land x = a) = (\exists x\, :\, \iota,\; \exists h\text{ proving }(x \in s^{\mathrm c}),\; (x\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'} \land x = a)\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(x\) in \(\iota\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(x \in s^{\mathrm c}\).
By definition, we obtain \(((x\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'} \land x = a) = ((x\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'} \land x = a)\).
By \paperlink{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}}, we obtain \((x\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'} \land x = a \iff (x\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'} \land x = a\).
\end{proofstep}
By definition, we obtain \(\left(x \in s^{\mathrm c}\right) = \left(x \in s^{\mathrm c}\right)\).
By \paperlink{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4966662e6f665f6571}}, we obtain \(x \in s^{\mathrm c} \iff x \in s^{\mathrm c}\).
By \paperlink{premise:6578697374735f70726f705f636f6e6772}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6578697374735f70726f705f636f6e6772}}, we obtain \((\exists h\text{ proving }(x \in s^{\mathrm c}),\; (x\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'} \land x = a) \iff (\exists h\text{ proving }(x \in s^{\mathrm c}),\; (x\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'} \land x = a)\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\exists h\text{ proving }(x \in s^{\mathrm c}),\; (x\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'} \land x = a) = (\exists h\text{ proving }(x \in s^{\mathrm c}),\; (x\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'} \land x = a)\).
By \paperlink{premise:6578697374735f616e645f7269676874}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6578697374735f616e645f7269676874}} with \(x \in s^{\mathrm c}\) as its type parameter \(\alpha\), we obtain \((\exists x_{1}\, :\, x \in s^{\mathrm c},\; (x_{2} \mapsto (x\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'})(x_{1}) \land x = a) \iff (\exists x_{1}\, :\, x \in s^{\mathrm c},\; (x_{2} \mapsto (x\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'})(x_{1})) \land x = a\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\exists x_{1}\text{ proving }(x \in s^{\mathrm c}),\; (x\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'} \land x = a) = ((\exists x_{1}\text{ proving }(x \in s^{\mathrm c}),\; (x\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'}) \land x = a)\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\exists h\text{ proving }(x \in s^{\mathrm c}),\; (x\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'} \land x = a) = ((\exists x_{1}\text{ proving }(x \in s^{\mathrm c}),\; (x\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'}) \land x = a)\).
\end{proofstep}
By function extensionality, we obtain \((x \mapsto \exists h\text{ proving }(x \in s^{\mathrm c}),\; (x\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'} \land x = a) = (x \mapsto (\exists x_{1}\text{ proving }(x \in s^{\mathrm c}),\; (x\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'}) \land x = a)\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((\exists x\, :\, \iota,\; \exists h\text{ proving }(x \in s^{\mathrm c}),\; (x\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'} \land x = a) = (\exists x\, :\, \iota,\; (\exists x_{1}\text{ proving }(x \in s^{\mathrm c}),\; (x\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'}) \land x = a)\).
By \paperlink{premise:6578697374735f65715f7269676874}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6578697374735f65715f7269676874}}, we obtain \((\exists a_{1}\, :\, \iota,\; (a_{2} \mapsto \exists x\text{ proving }(a_{2} \in s^{\mathrm c}),\; (a_{2}\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'})(a_{1}) \land a_{1} = a) \iff (a_{1} \mapsto \exists x\text{ proving }(a_{1} \in s^{\mathrm c}),\; (a_{1}\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'})(a)\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\exists a_{1}\, :\, \iota,\; (\exists x\text{ proving }(a_{1} \in s^{\mathrm c}),\; (a_{1}\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'}) \land a_{1} = a) = (\exists x\text{ proving }(a \in s^{\mathrm c}),\; (a\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'})\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\exists x\, :\, \iota,\; \exists h\text{ proving }(x \in s^{\mathrm c}),\; (x\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'} \land x = a) = (\exists x\text{ proving }(a \in s^{\mathrm c}),\; (a\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'})\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\exists x\, :\, s^{\mathrm c},\; x \in s^{'} \land x = a) = (\exists x\text{ proving }(a \in s^{\mathrm c}),\; (a\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'})\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((a \in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]) = (\exists x\text{ proving }(a \in s^{\mathrm c}),\; (a\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'})\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((a \notin (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]) = (\neg (\exists x\text{ proving }(a \in s^{\mathrm c}),\; (a\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'}))\).
By \paperlink{premise:6e6f745f657869737473}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6e6f745f657869737473}} with \(a \in s^{\mathrm c}\) as its type parameter \(\alpha\), we obtain \(\neg (\exists x\, :\, a \in s^{\mathrm c},\; (x_{1} \mapsto (a\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'})(x)) \iff (\forall x\, :\, a \in s^{\mathrm c},\; \neg (x_{1} \mapsto (a\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'})(x))\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((\neg (\exists x\text{ proving }(a \in s^{\mathrm c}),\; (a\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \in s^{'})) = (\forall x\, :\, a \in s^{\mathrm c},\; (a\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \notin s^{'})\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((a \notin (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]) = (\forall x\, :\, a \in s^{\mathrm c},\; (a\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \notin s^{'})\).
By \paperlink{premise:696d706c6965735f636f6e6772}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:696d706c6965735f636f6e6772}} with \(p_{1} = a \in s\) and \(p_{2} = a \in s\) and \(q_{1} = a \notin \left(z \mapsto z\right)\left[s^{'}\right]\) and \(q_{2} = \forall x\, :\, a \in s^{\mathrm c},\; \left(a\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}\right) \notin s^{'}\), we obtain \((a \in s \implies a \notin (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]) = (a \in s \implies (\forall x\, :\, a \in s^{\mathrm c},\; (a\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}) \notin s^{'}))\).
\end{proofstep}
Fix \(a\) in \(\iota\).
Assume \(a \in s\).
Assume \(a \in s^{\mathrm c}\).
Assume \(\left(a\, :\, s^{\mathrm c}\right) \in s^{'}\).
Use the hypothesis \(a \in s\).
Use the hypothesis \(a \in s^{\mathrm c}\).
The assertion contradicts its negation. Hence \(\mathrm{False}\).

Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f756e696f6e5f6f665f6469736a6f696e74}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f756e696f6e5f6f665f6469736a6f696e74}} (using \(\operatorname{Disjoint}\left(s, \left(z \mapsto z\right)\left[s^{'}\right]\right)\)), \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f696d6167655f6f665f696e6a656374697665}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f696d6167655f6f665f696e6a656374697665}}, \paperlink{premise:537562747970652e636f655f696e6a656374697665}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:537562747970652e636f655f696e6a656374697665}} and \paperlink{premise:4e61742e6164645f7375625f63616e63656c5f6c656674}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e6164645f7375625f63616e63656c5f6c656674}} gives \(\left|s^{'}\right| = \left|s \cup \left(z \mapsto z\right)\left[s^{'}\right]\right| - \left|s\right|\).
Applying the hypothesis \(\forall s_{1}\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota),\; |s_{1}| \le |\bigcup_{i\in s_{1}} t(i)|\), we obtain \(|s \cup (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]| \le |\bigcup_{i\in s \cup (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]} t(i)|\).
By \paperlink{premise:4e61742e7375625f6c655f7375625f7269676874}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e7375625f6c655f7375625f7269676874}} with \(k = \left|\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)\right|\), we obtain \(|s \cup (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]| - |\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)| \le |\bigcup_{i\in s \cup (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]} t(i)| - |\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)|\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.

\begin{proofstep}

By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((a \in s \cup (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]) = (a \in s \lor a \in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}])\).
By \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f696d616765}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f696d616765}} with \(s^{\mathrm c}\) as its type parameter \(\alpha\), we obtain \(a \in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}] \iff (\exists a_{1}\, :\, s^{\mathrm c},\; a_{1} \in s^{'} \land (z \mapsto z)(a_{1}) = a)\).
By propositional extensionality, we obtain \((a \in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]) = (\exists a_{1}\, :\, s^{\mathrm c},\; a_{1} \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = a)\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((a \in s \lor a \in (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]) = (a \in s \lor (\exists a_{1}\, :\, s^{\mathrm c},\; a_{1} \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = a))\).

Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((a \in s \cup (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}] \land t_{1} \in t(a)) = ((a \in s \lor (\exists a_{1}\, :\, s^{\mathrm c},\; a_{1} \in s^{'} \land a_{1} = a)) \land t_{1} \in t(a))\).

Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((t_{1} \in t(\operatorname{val}(a)) \setminus (\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i))) = (t_{1} \in t(\operatorname{val}(a)) \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota,\; \neg (x \in s \land t_{1} \in t(x))))\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((a \in s^{'} \land t_{1} \in t(\operatorname{val}(a)) \setminus (\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i))) = (a \in s^{'} \land (t_{1} \in t(\operatorname{val}(a)) \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota,\; \neg (x \in s \land t_{1} \in t(x)))))\).

Using the existential hypothesis, we obtain \(t_{1} \in t(x) \implies (\forall x_{1}\, :\, \iota,\; \neg (x_{1} \in s \land t_{1} \in t(x_{1}))) \implies (\exists a_{3}\, :\, s^{\mathrm c},\; a_{3} \in s^{'} \land (t_{1} \in t(a_{3}) \land (\forall x_{1}\, :\, \iota,\; \neg (x_{1} \in s \land t_{1} \in t(x_{1})))))\).
