By \paperlink{premise:6c655f72666c}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6c655f72666c}} with \(\mathbb{N}\) as its type parameter \(\alpha\), we obtain \(|\bigcup_{x^{'}\in s^{'}} (t(x^{'}) \setminus (\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)))| \le |\bigcup_{x^{'}\in s^{'}} (t(x^{'}) \setminus (\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)))|\).
By \paperlink{premise:4c452e6c652e7472616e73}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4c452e6c652e7472616e73}} with \(\mathbb{N}\) as its type parameter \(\alpha\), we obtain \(|(\bigcup_{i\in s \cup (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]} t(i)) \setminus (\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i))| \le |\bigcup_{x^{'}\in s^{'}} (t(x^{'}) \setminus (\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)))|\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f7364696666}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f7364696666}}, \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6269556e696f6e5f7375627365745f6269556e696f6e5f6f665f7375627365745f6c656674}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6269556e696f6e5f7375627365745f6269556e696f6e5f6f665f7375627365745f6c656674}} and \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e7375627365745f756e696f6e5f6c656674}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e7375627365745f756e696f6e5f6c656674}} in the preceding result gives \(|\bigcup_{i\in s \cup (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]} t(i)| - |\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)| \le |\bigcup_{x^{'}\in s^{'}} (t(x^{'}) \setminus (\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)))|\).
By \paperlink{premise:4c452e6c652e7472616e73}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4c452e6c652e7472616e73}} with \(\mathbb{N}\) as its type parameter \(\alpha\), we obtain \(|s \cup (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]| - |\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)| \le |\bigcup_{x^{'}\in s^{'}} (t(x^{'}) \setminus (\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)))|\).
Rewriting with \(\left|s\right| = \left|\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)\right|\) in the preceding result gives \(|s \cup (z \mapsto z)[s^{'}]| - |s| \le |\bigcup_{x^{'}\in s^{'}} (t(x^{'}) \setminus (\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)))|\).
Rewriting with \(\left|s^{'}\right| = \left|s \cup \left(z \mapsto z\right)\left[s^{'}\right]\right| - \left|s\right|\) in the preceding result gives \(|s^{'}| \le |\bigcup_{x^{'}\in s^{'}} (t(x^{'}) \setminus (\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)))|\).
\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}[{Second case of the inductive step}]\label{theorem:5}
Let \(\iota\) and \(\alpha\) be types. Assume equality on \(\alpha\) is decidable. Let \(t\) be a family of finite subsets of \(\alpha\) indexed by \(\iota\). Fix a finite enumeration of \(\iota\). Let \(n\) be a natural number. Assume \(\left|\iota\right| = n + 1\). Assume \(\forall s\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota),\; \left|s\right| \le \left|\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)\right|\). Assume as induction hypothesis that \(\forall \iota^{'}\text{ with finite enumeration }\mathit{inst}_{2},\; \forall t^{'}\, :\, \iota^{'} \to \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\alpha),\; |\iota^{'}| \le n \implies (\forall s^{'}\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota^{'}),\; |s^{'}| \le |\bigcup_{i\in s^{'}} t^{'}(i)|) \implies (\exists f\, :\, \iota^{'} \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota^{'},\; f(x) \in t^{'}(x)))\). Let \(s\) be a finite subset of \(\iota\). Assume \(s\ne\varnothing\). Assume \(s \ne \iota\). Assume \(\left|s\right| = \left|\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)\right|\). Then
\begin{center}
\(\displaystyle \exists f\, :\, \iota \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota,\; f(x) \in t(x)).\)
\end{center}
\end{lemma}

\begin{proof}
By \paperlink{premise:46696e747970652e636172645f636f65}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e747970652e636172645f636f65}}, we obtain \(\left|s\right| = \left|s\right|\).
By \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f6c745f6966665f6e655f756e6976}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f6c745f6966665f6e655f756e6976}}, we obtain \(\left|s\right| < \left|\iota\right| \iff s \ne \iota\).
Use the hypothesis \(s \ne \iota\).
By the reverse implication, we obtain \(\left|s\right| < \left|\iota\right|\).
By transitivity of the displayed relations, we obtain \(\left|s\right| < \left|\iota\right|\).
Use the hypothesis \(\left|\iota\right| = n + 1\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:4e61742e6164645f6f6e65}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e6164645f6f6e65}} in the preceding result gives \(\left|\iota\right| = n + 1\).
By transitivity of the displayed relations, we obtain \(\left|s\right| < n + 1\).
By \paperlink{premise:4e61742e6c655f6f665f6c745f73756363}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e6c655f6f665f6c745f73756363}}, we obtain \(\left|s\right| \le n\).
Set \(t^{'}\) equal to \(x^{'} \mapsto t\left(x^{'}\right)\).
Use the previously proved fact \(\left|s\right| \le n\).
By \paperlink{theorem:3}{Lemma~\ref{theorem:3}}, we obtain \(\forall s^{'}\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(s),\; |s^{'}| \le |\bigcup_{a^{'}\in s^{'}} t(a^{'})|\).
Applying the induction hypothesis, we obtain \(\exists f\, :\, s \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, s,\; f(x) \in t^{'}(x))\).
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(f^{'}\) in \(s \to \alpha\).
Assume \(f^{'}\text{ is injective} \land \left(\forall x\, :\, s,\; f^{'}\left(x\right) \in t^{'}\left(x\right)\right)\).
Use the hypothesis \(f^{'}\text{ is injective} \land \left(\forall x\, :\, s,\; f^{'}\left(x\right) \in t^{'}\left(x\right)\right)\).
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(f^{'}\text{ is injective}\).
Assume \(\forall x\, :\, s,\; f^{'}\left(x\right) \in t^{'}\left(x\right)\).
Set \(\iota^{''}\) equal to \(s^{\mathrm c}\).
Set \(t^{''}\) equal to \(a^{''} \mapsto t\left(a^{''}\right) \setminus \left(\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)\right)\).
Use the hypothesis \(s\ne\varnothing\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f636f6d706c5f6c745f6966665f6e6f6e656d707479}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f636f6d706c5f6c745f6966665f6e6f6e656d707479}} in the preceding result gives \(\left|s^{\mathrm c}\right| < \left|\iota\right|\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e747970652e636172645f636f65}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e747970652e636172645f636f65}} in the preceding result gives \(\left|s^{\mathrm c}\right| < \left|\iota\right|\).
By the preceding argument, we obtain \(\left|s^{\mathrm c}\right| < \left|\iota\right|\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e747970652e636172645f636f6e677227}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e747970652e636172645f636f6e677227}} and \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e636f655f636f6d706c}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e636f655f636f6d706c}} in the preceding result gives \(\left|s^{\mathrm c}\right| < \left|\iota\right|\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:4e61742e6164645f6f6e65}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e6164645f6f6e65}}, \(\left|\iota\right| = n + 1\) and substitution of equals in the preceding result gives \(\left|s^{\mathrm c}\right| < n + 1\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:4e61742e6c745f737563635f696666}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e6c745f737563635f696666}} in the preceding result gives \(\left|s^{\mathrm c}\right| \le n\).
By the preceding argument, we obtain \(\left|\iota^{''}\right| \le n\).
Use the previously proved fact \(\left|\iota^{''}\right| \le n\).
By \paperlink{theorem:4}{Lemma~\ref{theorem:4}}, we obtain \(\forall s^{'}\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(s^{\mathrm c}),\; |s^{'}| \le |\bigcup_{x^{'}\in s^{'}} (t(x^{'}) \setminus (\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)))|\).
Applying the induction hypothesis, we obtain \(\exists f\, :\, \iota^{''} \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota^{''},\; f(x) \in t^{''}(x))\).
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(f^{''}\) in \(\iota^{''} \to \alpha\).
Assume \(f^{''}\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota^{''},\; f^{''}(x) \in t^{''}(x))\).
Use the hypothesis \(f^{''}\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota^{''},\; f^{''}(x) \in t^{''}(x))\).
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(f^{''}\text{ is injective}\).
Assume \(\forall x\, :\, \iota^{''},\; f^{''}\left(x\right) \in t^{''}\left(x\right)\).
Fix \(x^{'}\) in \(\iota\).
Assume \(x^{'} \in s\).
Choose the witness \(x^{'}\).
Use the hypothesis \(x^{'} \in s\).
Applying the hypothesis \(\forall x\, :\, s,\; f^{'}\left(x\right) \in t^{'}\left(x\right)\), we obtain \(f^{'}\left(\left(x^{'}\, :\, s\right)\right) \in t^{'}\left(\left(x^{'}\, :\, s\right)\right)\).
Combining the two conclusions, we obtain \(x^{'} \in s \land f^{'}\left(\left(x^{'}\, :\, s\right)\right) \in t\left(x^{'}\right)\).
This witness proves existence. Hence \(\exists a\, :\, \iota,\; a \in s \land f^{'}\left(\left(x^{'}\, :\, s\right)\right) \in t\left(a\right)\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6269556e696f6e}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6269556e696f6e}} in the preceding result gives \(f^{'}\left(\left(x^{'}\, :\, s\right)\right) \in \bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)\).
Fix \(x^{''}\) in \(\iota\).
Assume \(x^{''} \notin s\).
Applying the hypothesis \(\forall x\, :\, \iota^{''},\; f^{''}\left(x\right) \in t^{''}\left(x\right)\), we obtain \(f^{''}((x^{''}\, :\, \iota^{''})) \in t^{''}((x^{''}\, :\, \iota^{''}))\).
Use the previously proved fact \(f^{''}((x^{''}\, :\, \iota^{''})) \in t^{''}((x^{''}\, :\, \iota^{''}))\), which simplifies to \(f^{''}((x^{''}\, :\, \iota^{''})) \in t(x^{''}) \setminus (\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i))\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f7364696666}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f7364696666}} in the preceding result gives \(f^{''}((x^{''}\, :\, \iota^{''})) \in t(x^{''}) \land f^{''}((x^{''}\, :\, \iota^{''})) \notin \bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)\).
Taking the second conjunct, we obtain \(f^{''}\left(\left(x^{''}\, :\, \iota^{''}\right)\right) \notin \bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)\).
Fix \(x^{'}\) in \(\iota\).
Fix \(x^{''}\) in \(\iota\).
Assume \(x^{'} \in s\).
Assume \(x^{''} \notin s\).
Assume \(f^{'}\left(\left(x^{'}\, :\, s\right)\right) = f^{''}\left(\left(x^{''}\, :\, \iota^{''}\right)\right)\).
Use the hypothesis \(x^{''} \notin s\).
Use the hypothesis \(x^{'} \in s\).
Applying the hypothesis \(\forall x^{'}\, :\, \iota,\; \forall h_{6}\, :\, x^{'} \in s,\; f^{'}((x^{'}\, :\, s)) \in \bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)\), we obtain \(f^{'}\left(\left(x^{'}\, :\, s\right)\right) \in \bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)\).
Rewriting with \(f^{'}\left(\left(x^{'}\, :\, s\right)\right) = f^{''}\left(\left(x^{''}\, :\, \iota^{''}\right)\right)\) in the preceding result gives \(f^{''}\left(\left(x^{''}\, :\, \iota^{''}\right)\right) \in \bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)\).
Applying the hypothesis \(\forall x^{''}\, :\, \iota,\; \forall h_{6}\, :\, x^{''} \notin s,\; f^{''}((x^{''}\, :\, \iota^{''})) \notin \bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)\), we obtain \(\mathrm{False}\).
Choose the witness \(x \mapsto \begin{cases}f^{'}((x\, :\, s)) &\text{given } h_{6}\text{ proving } x \in s\\ f^{''}((x\, :\, \iota^{''})) &\text{given } h_{6}\text{ proving } \neg (x \in s)\end{cases}\).
Use the hypothesis \(f^{'}\text{ is injective}\).
Use the hypothesis \(f^{''}\text{ is injective}\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(x\) in \(\iota\).
Fix \(x^{'}\) in \(\iota\).
Assume \(x \in s\).
Assume \(x^{'} \notin s\).
Use the hypothesis \(x \in s\).
Use the hypothesis \(x^{'} \notin s\).
Applying the hypothesis \(\forall x^{'}\, :\, \iota,\; \forall x^{''}\, :\, \iota,\; \forall h_{6}\, :\, x^{'} \in s,\; \forall h_{7}\, :\, x^{''} \notin s,\; f^{'}((x^{'}\, :\, s)) \ne f^{''}((x^{''}\, :\, \iota^{''}))\), we obtain \(f^{'}\left(\left(x\, :\, s\right)\right) \ne f^{''}\left(\left(x^{'}\, :\, \iota^{''}\right)\right)\).
\end{proofstep}
By \paperlink{premise:46756e6374696f6e2e496e6a6563746976652e64697465}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46756e6374696f6e2e496e6a6563746976652e64697465}} with \(p = y \mapsto y \in s\), we obtain \((x \mapsto \begin{cases}f^{'}((x\, :\, s)) &\text{given } h_{6}\text{ proving } x \in s\\ f^{''}((x\, :\, \{a\in \iota \mid a \notin s\})) &\text{given } h_{6}\text{ proving } \neg (x \in s)\end{cases})\text{ is injective}\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(x\) in \(\iota\).
Distinguish whether \(x \in s\) holds.
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(x \in s\).
Applying the hypothesis \(\forall x\, :\, s,\; f^{'}\left(x\right) \in t^{'}\left(x\right)\), we obtain \(f^{'}\left(\left(x\, :\, s\right)\right) \in t\left(x\right)\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:6469665f706f73}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6469665f706f73}} (using \(x \in s\)) in the preceding result gives \(\begin{cases}f^{'}((x\, :\, s)) &\text{given } h_{7}\text{ proving } x \in s\\ f^{''}((x\, :\, \iota^{''})) &\text{given } h_{7}\text{ proving } \neg (x \in s)\end{cases} \in t(x)\).
\end{proofstep}
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(x \notin s\).
Applying the hypothesis \(\forall x\, :\, \iota^{''},\; f^{''}\left(x\right) \in t^{''}\left(x\right)\), we obtain \(f^{''}\left(\left(x\, :\, \iota^{''}\right)\right) \in t^{''}\left(\left(x\, :\, \iota^{''}\right)\right)\).
By \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e73646966665f737562736574}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e73646966665f737562736574}}, we obtain \(f^{''}\left(\left(x\, :\, \iota^{''}\right)\right) \in t\left(x\right)\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:6469665f6e6567}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6469665f6e6567}} (using \(x \notin s\)) in the preceding result gives \(\begin{cases}f^{'}((x\, :\, s)) &\text{given } h_{7}\text{ proving } x \in s\\ f^{''}((x\, :\, \iota^{''})) &\text{given } h_{7}\text{ proving } \neg (x \in s)\end{cases} \in t(x)\).
\end{proofstep}
The proposition or its negation holds. Hence \(\begin{cases}f^{'}((x\, :\, s)) &\text{given } h_{6}\text{ proving } x \in s\\ f^{''}((x\, :\, \iota^{''})) &\text{given } h_{6}\text{ proving } \neg (x \in s)\end{cases} \in t(x)\).
By the preceding argument, we obtain \(\begin{cases}f^{'}((x\, :\, s)) &\text{given } h_{6}\text{ proving } x \in s\\ f^{''}((x\, :\, \iota^{''})) &\text{given } h_{6}\text{ proving } \neg (x \in s)\end{cases} \in t(x)\).
\end{proofstep}
Combining the two conclusions, we obtain \((x \mapsto \begin{cases}f^{'}((x\, :\, s)) &\text{given } h_{6}\text{ proving } x \in s\\ f^{''}((x\, :\, \iota^{''})) &\text{given } h_{6}\text{ proving } \neg (x \in s)\end{cases})\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota,\; \begin{cases}f^{'}((x\, :\, s)) &\text{given } h_{6}\text{ proving } x \in s\\ f^{''}((x\, :\, \iota^{''})) &\text{given } h_{6}\text{ proving } \neg (x \in s)\end{cases} \in t(x))\).
This witness proves existence. Hence \(\exists f\, :\, \iota \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota,\; f(x) \in t(x))\).
\end{proofstep}
Using the two conjuncts, we obtain \(\exists f\, :\, \iota \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota,\; f(x) \in t(x))\).
\end{proofstep}
Using the existential hypothesis, we obtain \(\exists f\, :\, \iota \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota,\; f(x) \in t(x))\).
\end{proofstep}
Using the two conjuncts, we obtain \(\exists f\, :\, \iota \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota,\; f(x) \in t(x))\).
\end{proofstep}
Using the existential hypothesis, we obtain \(\exists f\, :\, \iota \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota,\; f(x) \in t(x))\).
\end{proof}

\section{Hall marriage theorem}
\begin{lemma}[{Hall hard inductive}]\label{theorem:6}
Let \(\iota\) and \(\alpha\) be types. Assume equality on \(\alpha\) is decidable. Let \(t\) be a family of finite subsets of \(\alpha\) indexed by \(\iota\). Assume \(\iota\) is finite. Assume \(\forall s\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota),\; \left|s\right| \le \left|\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)\right|\). Then
\begin{center}
\(\displaystyle \exists f\, :\, \iota \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota,\; f(x) \in t(x)).\)
\end{center}
\end{lemma}

\begin{proof}
Use the hypothesis \(\operatorname{Finite}\left(\iota\right)\).
By \paperlink{premise:6e6f6e656d7074795f66696e74797065}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6e6f6e656d7074795f66696e74797065}} with \(\iota\) as its type parameter \(\alpha\), we obtain \(\operatorname{Fintype}\left(\iota\right)\text{ is nonempty}\).
Use the hypothesis \(\operatorname{Finite}\left(\iota\right)\).
By \paperlink{premise:6e6f6e656d7074795f66696e74797065}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:6e6f6e656d7074795f66696e74797065}} with \(\iota\) as its type parameter \(\alpha\), we obtain \(\operatorname{Fintype}\left(\iota\right)\text{ is nonempty}\).
By definition, we obtain \(x \mapsto y \mapsto x = y\).
Consider the possible forms of \(\operatorname{Fintype}\left(\iota\right)\text{ is nonempty}\).
\emph{Intro case.}
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(\mathit{val}\) in \(\operatorname{Fintype}\left(\iota\right)\).
Assume \(x \mapsto y \mapsto x = y\).
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(n\) in \(\mathbb{N}\).
Assume as induction hypothesis that \(\forall m\, :\, \mathbb{N},\; m < n \implies (\forall \iota_{1}\, :\, \mathsf{Type},\; \forall t_{1}\, :\, \iota_{1} \to \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\alpha),\; \operatorname{Finite}(\iota_{1}) \implies (\forall s\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota_{1}),\; |s| \le |\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)|) \implies (\forall \mathit{val}_{1}\, :\, \operatorname{Fintype}(\iota_{1}),\; |\iota_{1}| = m \implies (\exists f\, :\, \iota_{1} \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota_{1},\; f(x) \in t_{1}(x)))))\).
Fix \(\iota_{1}\) in \(\mathsf{Type}\).
Fix \(t_{1}\) in \(\iota_{1} \to \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\alpha)\).
Assume \(\operatorname{Finite}\left(\iota_{1}\right)\).
Assume \(\forall s\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota_{1}),\; |s| \le |\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)|\).
Fix \(\mathit{val}_{1}\) in \(\operatorname{Fintype}\left(\iota_{1}\right)\).
Assume \(\left|\iota_{1}\right| = n\).
In the zero case,
\begin{proofstep}
Assume as induction hypothesis that \(\forall m\, :\, \mathbb{N},\; m < 0 \implies (\forall \iota_{2}\, :\, \mathsf{Type},\; \forall t_{2}\, :\, \iota_{2} \to \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\alpha),\; \operatorname{Finite}(\iota_{2}) \implies (\forall s\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota_{2}),\; |s| \le |\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{2}(i)|) \implies (\forall \mathit{val}_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Fintype}(\iota_{2}),\; |\iota_{2}| = m \implies (\exists f\, :\, \iota_{2} \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota_{2},\; f(x) \in t_{2}(x)))))\).
Assume \(\left|\iota_{1}\right| = 0\).
Choose the witness \(\text{the unique function from the empty type }\iota_{1}\text{ to }\alpha\).
Fix \(a\) in \(\iota_{1}\).
Use the hypothesis \(\left|\iota_{1}\right| = 0\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e747970652e636172645f65715f7a65726f5f696666}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e747970652e636172645f65715f7a65726f5f696666}} in the preceding result gives \(\iota_{1}\text{ is empty}\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(a\) in \(\iota_{1}\).
Use the hypothesis \(\left|\iota_{1}\right| = 0\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e747970652e636172645f65715f7a65726f5f696666}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e747970652e636172645f65715f7a65726f5f696666}} in the preceding result gives \(\iota_{1}\text{ is empty}\).
The element \(a\) cannot belong to the empty type \(\iota_{1}\). Hence \(\forall a_{2}\, :\, \iota_{1},\; \operatorname{empty\,map}_{\iota_{1}\to \alpha}(a) = \operatorname{empty\,map}_{\iota_{1}\to \alpha}(a_{2}) \implies a = a_{2}\).
\end{proofstep}
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(a\) in \(\iota_{1}\).
Use the hypothesis \(\left|\iota_{1}\right| = 0\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e747970652e636172645f65715f7a65726f5f696666}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e747970652e636172645f65715f7a65726f5f696666}} in the preceding result gives \(\iota_{1}\text{ is empty}\).
The element \(a\) cannot belong to the empty type \(\iota_{1}\). Hence \(\operatorname{empty\,map}_{\iota_{1}\to \alpha}(a) \in t_{1}\left(a\right)\).
\end{proofstep}
Combining the two conclusions, we obtain \((\text{the unique function from the empty type }\iota_{1}\text{ to }\alpha)\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota_{1},\; \operatorname{empty\,map}_{\iota_{1}\to \alpha}(x) \in t_{1}(x))\).
This witness proves existence. Hence \(\exists f\, :\, \iota_{1} \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota_{1},\; f(x) \in t_{1}(x))\).
\end{proofstep}
In the successor case,
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(n_{1}\) in \(\mathbb{N}\).
Assume as induction hypothesis that \(\forall m\, :\, \mathbb{N},\; m < n_{1} + 1 \implies (\forall \iota_{2}\, :\, \mathsf{Type},\; \forall t_{2}\, :\, \iota_{2} \to \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\alpha),\; \operatorname{Finite}(\iota_{2}) \implies (\forall s\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota_{2}),\; |s| \le |\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{2}(i)|) \implies (\forall \mathit{val}_{2}\, :\, \operatorname{Fintype}(\iota_{2}),\; |\iota_{2}| = m \implies (\exists f\, :\, \iota_{2} \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota_{2},\; f(x) \in t_{2}(x)))))\).
Assume \(\left|\iota_{1}\right| = n_{1} + 1\).
Fix \(\iota^{'}\) in \(\mathsf{Type}\).
Fix \(t^{'}\) in \(\iota^{'} \to \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\alpha)\).
Assume \(\left|\iota^{'}\right| \le n_{1}\).
Assume \(\forall s^{'}\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota^{'}),\; |s^{'}| \le |\bigcup_{i\in s^{'}} t^{'}(i)|\).
Use the hypothesis \(\left|\iota^{'}\right| \le n_{1}\).
By \paperlink{premise:4e61742e6c745f737563635f6f665f6c65}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e6c745f737563635f6f665f6c65}}, we obtain \(\left|\iota^{'}\right| < n_{1} + 1\).
By \paperlink{premise:46696e6974652e6f665f66696e74797065}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e6974652e6f665f66696e74797065}} with \(\iota^{'}\) as its type parameter \(\alpha\), we obtain \(\operatorname{Finite}\left(\iota^{'}\right)\).
Use the hypothesis \(\forall s^{'}\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota^{'}),\; |s^{'}| \le |\bigcup_{i\in s^{'}} t^{'}(i)|\).
By definition, we obtain \(\left|\iota^{'}\right| = \left|\iota^{'}\right|\).
Applying the induction hypothesis, we obtain \(\exists f\, :\, \iota^{'} \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota^{'},\; f(x) \in t^{'}(x))\).
Distinguish whether \(\forall s\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota_{1}),\; s\ne\varnothing \implies s \ne \iota_{1} \implies |s| < |\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)|\) holds.
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(\forall s\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota_{1}),\; s\ne\varnothing \implies s \ne \iota_{1} \implies |s| < |\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)|\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(\iota^{'}\) in \(\mathsf{Type}\).
Applying the hypothesis \(\forall \iota^{'}\text{ with finite enumeration }\mathit{inst}_{3},\; \forall t^{'}\, :\, \iota^{'} \to \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\alpha),\; |\iota^{'}| \le n_{1} \implies (\forall s^{'}\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota^{'}),\; |s^{'}| \le |\bigcup_{i\in s^{'}} t^{'}(i)|) \implies (\exists f\, :\, \iota^{'} \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota^{'},\; f(x) \in t^{'}(x)))\), we obtain \(\forall t^{'}\, :\, \iota^{'} \to \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\alpha),\; |\iota^{'}| \le n_{1} \implies (\forall s^{'}\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota^{'}),\; |s^{'}| \le |\bigcup_{i\in s^{'}} t^{'}(i)|) \implies (\exists f\, :\, \iota^{'} \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota^{'},\; f(x) \in t^{'}(x)))\).
\end{proofstep}
By \paperlink{theorem:2}{Lemma~\ref{theorem:2}}, we obtain \(\exists f\, :\, \iota_{1} \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota_{1},\; f(x) \in t_{1}(x))\).
\end{proofstep}
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(\neg (\forall s\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota_{1}),\; s\ne\varnothing \implies s \ne \iota_{1} \implies |s| < |\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)|)\).
The substitution uses the following argument.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(s\) in \(\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota_{1})\).
By \paperlink{premise:4d6174686c69622e5461637469632e507573684e65672e6e6f745f696d706c6965735f6571}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4d6174686c69622e5461637469632e507573684e65672e6e6f745f696d706c6965735f6571}} with \(p = s\ne\varnothing\) and \(q = s \ne \iota_{1} \implies \left|s\right| < \left|\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)\right|\), we obtain \((\neg (s\ne\varnothing \implies s \ne \iota_{1} \implies |s| < |\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)|)) = (s\ne\varnothing \land \neg (s \ne \iota_{1} \implies |s| < |\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)|))\).
By \paperlink{premise:4d6174686c69622e5461637469632e507573684e65672e6e6f745f696d706c6965735f6571}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4d6174686c69622e5461637469632e507573684e65672e6e6f745f696d706c6965735f6571}} with \(p = s \ne \iota_{1}\) and \(q = \left|s\right| < \left|\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)\right|\), we obtain \((\neg (s \ne \iota_{1} \implies |s| < |\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)|)) = (s \ne \iota_{1} \land \neg (|s| < |\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)|))\).
By \paperlink{premise:4d6174686c69622e5461637469632e507573684e65672e6e6f745f6c745f6571}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4d6174686c69622e5461637469632e507573684e65672e6e6f745f6c745f6571}} with \(\beta = \mathbb{N}\) and \(a = \left|s\right|\) and \(b = \left|\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)\right|\), we obtain \((\neg (|s| < |\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)|)) = (|\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)| \le |s|)\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((s \ne \iota_{1} \land \neg (|s| < |\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)|)) = (s \ne \iota_{1} \land |\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)| \le |s|)\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\neg (s \ne \iota_{1} \implies |s| < |\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)|)) = (s \ne \iota_{1} \land |\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)| \le |s|)\).
Applying the same function to equal arguments, we obtain \((s\ne\varnothing \land \neg (s \ne \iota_{1} \implies |s| < |\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)|)) = (s\ne\varnothing \land (s \ne \iota_{1} \land |\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)| \le |s|))\).
Chaining the equalities, we obtain \((\neg (s\ne\varnothing \implies s \ne \iota_{1} \implies |s| < |\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)|)) = (s\ne\varnothing \land (s \ne \iota_{1} \land |\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)| \le |s|))\).
\end{proofstep}
Use the hypothesis \(\neg (\forall s\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota_{1}),\; s\ne\varnothing \implies s \ne \iota_{1} \implies |s| < |\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)|)\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:4d6174686c69622e5461637469632e507573684e65672e6e6f745f666f72616c6c5f6571}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4d6174686c69622e5461637469632e507573684e65672e6e6f745f666f72616c6c5f6571}}, \paperlink{premise:66756e657874}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:66756e657874}} and the identity established above in the preceding result gives \(\exists s\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota_{1}),\; s\ne\varnothing \land (s \ne \iota_{1} \land |\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)| \le |s|)\).
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(s\) in \(\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota_{1})\).
Assume \(s\ne\varnothing \land (s \ne \iota_{1} \land |\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)| \le |s|)\).
Use the hypothesis \(s\ne\varnothing \land (s \ne \iota_{1} \land |\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)| \le |s|)\).
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(s\ne\varnothing\).
Assume \(s \ne \iota_{1} \land \left|\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)\right| \le \left|s\right|\).
Use the hypothesis \(s \ne \iota_{1} \land \left|\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)\right| \le \left|s\right|\).
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(s \ne \iota_{1}\).
Assume \(\left|\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)\right| \le \left|s\right|\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(\iota^{'}\) in \(\mathsf{Type}\).
Applying the hypothesis \(\forall \iota^{'}\text{ with finite enumeration }\mathit{inst}_{3},\; \forall t^{'}\, :\, \iota^{'} \to \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\alpha),\; |\iota^{'}| \le n_{1} \implies (\forall s^{'}\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota^{'}),\; |s^{'}| \le |\bigcup_{i\in s^{'}} t^{'}(i)|) \implies (\exists f\, :\, \iota^{'} \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota^{'},\; f(x) \in t^{'}(x)))\), we obtain \(\forall t^{'}\, :\, \iota^{'} \to \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\alpha),\; |\iota^{'}| \le n_{1} \implies (\forall s^{'}\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota^{'}),\; |s^{'}| \le |\bigcup_{i\in s^{'}} t^{'}(i)|) \implies (\exists f\, :\, \iota^{'} \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota^{'},\; f(x) \in t^{'}(x)))\).
\end{proofstep}
Applying the hypothesis \(\forall s\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota_{1}),\; |s| \le |\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)|\), we obtain \(\left|s\right| \le \left|\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)\right|\).
Use the hypothesis \(\left|\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)\right| \le \left|s\right|\).
By \paperlink{premise:4e61742e6c655f616e746973796d6d}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:4e61742e6c655f616e746973796d6d}}, we obtain \(\left|s\right| = \left|\bigcup_{i\in s} t_{1}(i)\right|\).
By \paperlink{theorem:5}{Lemma~\ref{theorem:5}}, we obtain \(\exists f\, :\, \iota_{1} \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota_{1},\; f(x) \in t_{1}(x))\).
\end{proofstep}
Using the two conjuncts, we obtain \(\exists f\, :\, \iota_{1} \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota_{1},\; f(x) \in t_{1}(x))\).
\end{proofstep}
Using the two conjuncts, we obtain \(\exists f\, :\, \iota_{1} \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota_{1},\; f(x) \in t_{1}(x))\).
\end{proofstep}
Using the existential hypothesis, we obtain \(\exists f\, :\, \iota_{1} \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota_{1},\; f(x) \in t_{1}(x))\).
\end{proofstep}
The proposition or its negation holds. Hence \(\exists f\, :\, \iota_{1} \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota_{1},\; f(x) \in t_{1}(x))\).
\end{proofstep}
Use the induction hypothesis.
Use the hypothesis \(\left|\iota_{1}\right| = n\).
The zero and successor cases exhaust the natural numbers. Hence \(\exists f\, :\, \iota_{1} \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota_{1},\; f(x) \in t_{1}(x))\).
\end{proofstep}
Use the hypothesis \(\operatorname{Finite}\left(\iota\right)\).
Use the hypothesis \(\forall s\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota),\; \left|s\right| \le \left|\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)\right|\).
By definition, we obtain \(\left|\iota\right| = \left|\iota\right|\).
By strong induction on the natural number, we obtain \(\exists f\, :\, \iota \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota,\; f(x) \in t(x))\).
\end{proofstep}
This proves \(\exists f\, :\, \iota \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota,\; f(x) \in t(x))\).
\end{proof}

\section{Results}
\begin{theorem}[{Hall's Marriage Theorem}]\label{theorem:7}
Let \(\iota\) and \(\alpha\) be types. Assume \(\iota\) is finite. Assume equality on \(\alpha\) is decidable. Let \(t\) be a family of finite subsets of \(\alpha\) indexed by \(\iota\). Then
\begin{center}
\(\displaystyle (\forall s\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota),\; |s| \le |\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)|) \iff (\exists f\, :\, \iota \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota,\; f(x) \in t(x))).\)
\end{center}
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
By \paperlink{theorem:6}{Lemma~\ref{theorem:6}}, we obtain \((\forall s\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota),\; |s| \le |\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)|) \implies (\exists f\, :\, \iota \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota,\; f(x) \in t(x)))\).
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(\exists f\, :\, \iota \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota,\; f(x) \in t(x))\).
Use the hypothesis \(\exists f\, :\, \iota \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota,\; f(x) \in t(x))\).
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(f\) in \(\iota \to \alpha\).
Assume \(f\text{ is injective} \land \left(\forall x\, :\, \iota,\; f\left(x\right) \in t\left(x\right)\right)\).
Use the hypothesis \(f\text{ is injective} \land \left(\forall x\, :\, \iota,\; f\left(x\right) \in t\left(x\right)\right)\).
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(f\text{ is injective}\).
Assume \(\forall x\, :\, \iota,\; f\left(x\right) \in t\left(x\right)\).
Fix \(s\) in \(\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota)\).
For the required implication, argue as follows.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(a_{1}\) in \(\alpha\).
Assume \(\exists a_{3}\, :\, \iota,\; a_{3} \in s \land f\left(a_{3}\right) = a_{1}\).
Use the hypothesis \(\exists a_{3}\, :\, \iota,\; a_{3} \in s \land f\left(a_{3}\right) = a_{1}\).
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Fix \(x\) in \(\iota\).
Assume \(x \in s \land f\left(x\right) = a_{1}\).
Use the hypothesis \(x \in s \land f\left(x\right) = a_{1}\).
Consider the following case.
\begin{proofstep}
Assume \(x \in s\).
Assume \(f\left(x\right) = a_{1}\).
Choose the witness \(x\).
Use the hypothesis \(x \in s\).
Applying the hypothesis \(\forall x\, :\, \iota,\; f\left(x\right) \in t\left(x\right)\), we obtain \(f\left(x\right) \in t\left(x\right)\).
Combining the two conclusions, we obtain \(x \in s \land f\left(x\right) \in t\left(x\right)\).
This witness proves existence. Hence \(\exists a_{3}\, :\, \iota,\; a_{3} \in s \land f\left(x\right) \in t\left(a_{3}\right)\).
Use the hypothesis \(f\left(x\right) = a_{1}\).
Substituting equals, we obtain \(\exists a_{3}\, :\, \iota,\; a_{3} \in s \land a_{1} \in t\left(a_{3}\right)\).
\end{proofstep}
Using the two conjuncts, we obtain \(\exists a_{3}\, :\, \iota,\; a_{3} \in s \land a_{1} \in t\left(a_{3}\right)\).
\end{proofstep}
Using the existential hypothesis, we obtain \(\exists a_{3}\, :\, \iota,\; a_{3} \in s \land a_{1} \in t\left(a_{3}\right)\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6269556e696f6e}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f6269556e696f6e}} in the preceding result gives \((\exists a_{2}\, :\, \iota,\; a_{2} \in s \land f(a_{2}) = a_{1}) \implies a_{1} \in \bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f696d616765}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e6d656d5f696d616765}} in the preceding result gives \(a_{1} \in f\left[s\right] \implies a_{1} \in \bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)\).
\end{proofstep}
By \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f6c655f63617264}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f6c655f63617264}}, we obtain \(\left|f\left[s\right]\right| \le \left|\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)\right|\).
Rewriting with \paperlink{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f696d6167655f6f665f696e6a656374697665}{Prerequisite~P\ref{premise:46696e7365742e636172645f696d6167655f6f665f696e6a656374697665}} (using \(f\text{ is injective}\)) in the preceding result gives \(\left|s\right| \le \left|\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)\right|\).
\end{proofstep}
Using the two conjuncts, we obtain \(\forall s\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota),\; \left|s\right| \le \left|\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)\right|\).
\end{proofstep}
Using the existential hypothesis, we obtain \(\forall s\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota),\; \left|s\right| \le \left|\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)\right|\).
\end{proofstep}
Together the two implications give the equivalence. Hence \((\forall s\, :\, \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{fin}}(\iota),\; |s| \le |\bigcup_{i\in s} t(i)|) \iff (\exists f\, :\, \iota \to \alpha,\; f\text{ is injective} \land (\forall x\, :\, \iota,\; f(x) \in t(x)))\).
\end{proof}
